660-function mapLocalTrackToPlot(
661-  from: PlotPoint,
662-  to: PlotPoint,

678-    last.x - first.x;
679-
680-  const localDy =
681-    last.y - first.y;
682-

684-    Math.hypot(
685-      localDx,
686-      localDy,
687-    );
688-

690-    to.x - from.x;
691-
692-  const screenDy =
693-    to.y - from.y;

696-    Math.hypot(
697-      screenDx,
698-      screenDy,
699-    );
700-

702-    localLength < 1e-6 ||
703-    screenLength < 1e-6
704-  ) {
705-    return null;
706-  }
707-
708-  const localLengthSq =
709-    localLength *
710-    localLength;
711-
712-  const perpendicularX =
713-    -screenDy /
714-    screenLength;
715-
716-  const perpendicularY =
717-    screenDx /
718-    screenLength;
719-
720-  const lateralScale =
721-    screenLength /
722-    localLength;
723-
724-  return localPoints.map(

727-        point.x -
728-        first.x;
729-
730-      const dy =
731-        point.y -
732-        first.y;
733-
734-      /*
735-       * Position along the shot's local start→finish axis.
736-       */
737-      const along =
738-        (
739-          dx * localDx +
740-          dy * localDy
741-        ) /

744-      /*

888-        point,

899-          point.z,

660:function mapLocalTrackToPlot(
661-  from: PlotPoint,
662-  to: PlotPoint,
663-  localPoints: LocalTrackPoint[],

665-  if (localPoints.length < 2) {

667-  }
668-
669-  const first =
670-    localPoints[0];
671-
672-  const last =
673-    localPoints[
674-      localPoints.length - 1
675-    ];

677-  const localDx =
678-    last.x - first.x;
679-
680-  const localDy =
681-    last.y - first.y;
682-
683-  const localLength =
684-    Math.hypot(
685-      localDx,
686-      localDy,
687-    );
688-
689-  const screenDx =
690-    to.x - from.x;
691-
692-  const screenDy =
693-    to.y - from.y;

696-    Math.hypot(
697-      screenDx,
698-      screenDy,
699-    );

702-    localLength < 1e-6 ||
703-    screenLength < 1e-6
704-  ) {
705-    return null;
706-  }
707-

709-    localLength *
710-    localLength;
711-
712-  const perpendicularX =
713-    -screenDy /
714-    screenLength;
715-
716-  const perpendicularY =
717-    screenDx /
718-    screenLength;
719-
720-  const lateralScale =
721-    screenLength /
722-    localLength;
723-
724-  return localPoints.map(
725-    (point) => {
726-      const dx =
727-        point.x -
728-        first.x;
729-
730-      const dy =
731-        point.y -
732-        first.y;
733-
734-      /*
735-       * Position along the shot's local start→finish axis.
736-       */
737-      const along =
738-        (
739-          dx * localDx +
740-          dy * localDy
741-        ) /
